Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on physical structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 922. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 878 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to physical structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 922/878 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any physical module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 539. The projective resolution has length at most 716 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 539 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the physical structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 716. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
